\begin{split}|A_{2}|^{2}\leq&\ C\Big|\int_{t}^{T}(\alpha(r)-\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}}))\Delta_{\lambda}Ydr\Big|^{2}\\
&+C\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\left(\beta(r)-\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\right)\Delta_{\lambda}Zdr\Big|^{2}\\
&+C\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[(\tilde{g}(r)-\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}))\Delta_{\lambda}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}]dr\Big|^{2}.\end{split}

For the first two terms on the right hand side of the above inequality, by the Lipschitz continuity of
\partial_{(y,z)}f
and inequality (
208
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\,\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\left(\alpha(r)-\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\right)\Delta_{\lambda}Ydr\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\left(\beta(r)-\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\right)\Delta_{\lambda}Zdr\Big|^{2}\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}.

For the third term, we claim that

(215)

\mathbb{E}\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\tilde{g}(r)-\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}))\Delta_{\lambda}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}\right]dr\Big|^{2}\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4},

with
C
depending only on
\|\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}},

and therefore we have

(216)

\mathbb{E}|A_{2}|^{2}\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4},

in view of (
214
) and above estimates.
Indeed, by the Hölder inequality and estimate (
210
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[(\tilde{g}(r)-\partial_{\nu}f)\Delta_{\lambda}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}]dr\Big|^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}[\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}|\tilde{g}-\partial_{\nu}f|^{2}dr]\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}|\Delta_{\lambda}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}|^{2}dr

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathbb{E}\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}\Big|\int_{0}^{1}(\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma,\eta},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta^{\lambda\xi}}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta}+\theta(Y^{\eta^{\lambda\xi}}-Y^{\eta})},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}+\theta(\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}}-\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}))

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}))d\theta\Big|^{2}dr

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\|\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}}-\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|B^{\eta^{\lambda\xi}}-B^{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}})^{2}\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}.

For
A_{3},
from Lipschitz continuity of
\partial_{\mu_{t}}f
in
(\mu,\nu,\tilde{\omega})
, we have

(217)

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}|A_{3}|^{2}=&\ \mathbb{E}\big|\int_{t}^{T}\int_{0}^{1}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta+\theta(\eta^{\lambda\xi}-\eta)}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}+\theta(\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}-\tilde{\eta})})\\
&\ -\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})]\tilde{\xi}d\theta dr\big|^{2}\\
\leq&\ C\left|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[(\|\eta^{\lambda\xi}_{t}-\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}-\tilde{\eta}\|)|\tilde{\xi}|]\right|^{2}\\
\leq&\ C\left|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\lambda\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}\tilde{\xi}+\lambda|\tilde{\xi}|^{2}]\right|^{2}\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}.\end{split}

We now estimate
A_{4}
. Since

(218)

\Delta_{\lambda}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}-\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}\tilde{\xi}-\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\tilde{\xi}}=\ A_{41}+A_{42}

with

(219)

\begin{split}A_{41}&:=\ [\frac{1}{\lambda}(\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}},\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}}}-\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta},\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}}})-\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}\tilde{\xi}],\quad\quad\text{and}\\
A_{42}&:=[\frac{1}{\lambda}(\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta},\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\tilde{\xi}}}}-\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta},\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{\eta}}})-\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\tilde{\xi}}],\end{split}

then, from boundedness of
\partial_{\nu}f,
we have

(220)

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}|A_{4}|^{2}&=\mathbb{E}\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}\Big[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})(A_{41}(r)+A_{42}(r))\Big]dr\Big|^{2}\\
&\leq C\Big(\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[A_{41}]dr\Big|^{2}+\Big|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[A_{42}]dr\Big|^{2}\Big).\end{split}

From Proposition
4.6
, we have

(221)

\begin{split}\left|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[A_{41}]dr\right|^{2}&\leq C\int_{t}^{T}\left[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}\int_{0}^{1}|(\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\theta\tilde{\xi}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\lambda\xi}}}-\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta},\mathcal{L}_{\eta}})\tilde{\xi}|d\theta\right]^{2}dr\\
&\leq C\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\int_{t}^{T}\int_{0}^{1}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}|\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\lambda\theta\tilde{\xi}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\lambda\xi}}}-\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta},\mathcal{L}_{\eta}}|^{2}d\theta dr\\
&\leq C\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\int_{t}^{T}\int_{0}^{1}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}(\|\tilde{\eta}_{t}^{\lambda\theta\tilde{\xi}}-\tilde{\eta}_{t}\|)^{2}d\theta dr\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\end{split}

for a constant
C
only depending on
\|\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}.

Since

(222)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[A_{42}]dr\right|^{2}\leq\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}|A_{42}|^{2}dr\leq C\sup_{\gamma_{t}}\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}|\Delta_{\lambda}Y-\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}|^{2}dr,

for a constant
C
independent of
(\gamma,\eta),
we have

(223)

\mathbb{E}|A_{4}|^{2}\leq C\left(\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}+\sup_{\gamma_{t}}\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}|\Delta_{\lambda}Y-\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}|^{2}dr\right).

Finally, in view of inequalities (
213
), (
216
), (
217
), (
223
) and (
212
), we have

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{\lambda}Y-\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\leq C(\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}+\sup_{\gamma_{t}}\int_{t}^{T}\|\Delta_{\lambda}Y-\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{2}dr),

where
C
only depends on
\|\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}
.
Then, using Gronwall’s inequality, we have

(224)

\|\Delta_{\lambda}Y-\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\leq C\lambda^{2}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\rightarrow 0,\ \ \text{ as }\lambda\rightarrow 0.

∎

To show the strongly vertical differentiability of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}
in
\eta_{t},
in view of Definition
2.10
, for any
\tau\leq t
and
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{\tau},\mathbb{R}^{d}),
consider
\eta_{t}^{\tau,\lambda\xi}:=\eta_{t}+\lambda\xi 1_{[\tau,T]}
. Similar as the vertical differentiable case, we firstly need to show the following limit exits in
\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T]),

(225)

\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t},\xi}:=\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}\frac{1}{\lambda}(Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}^{\tau,\lambda\xi}}-Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}).

Indeed,
\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t},\xi}
is the unique solution of the following BSDE: for
s\in[t,T],

(226)

\begin{split}\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(s)&=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\Phi(B^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})(\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}\tilde{\xi}+\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\tilde{\xi}})(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ -\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(r)dB(r),\end{split}

where
\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}{Y}^{\eta_{t},\xi}
solves the following mean-field BSDE

(227)

\begin{split}\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},\xi}(s)&=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\eta_{t}},\xi}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})(\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}\tilde{\xi}+\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\tilde{\xi}})(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Z^{{\eta_{t}},\xi}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Z^{{\eta_{t}},\xi}(r)dB(r).\end{split}

According to Assumption
(H1)
, we see that BSDEs (
227
) and (
226
) are well-posed. Moreover, following a
similar argument as in Lemma
4.9
, for the Gâteaux strong vertical differentiability, we have

Lemma 4.10
.

\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is a bounded linear operator from
L^{2}({\mathcal{F}_{\tau}},\mathbb{R}^{d})
to
\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T])
. Moreover,
\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}
is the Gâteaux strong vertical derivative of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}
at
(\tau,t,\eta)
:

(228)

\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}=\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}\frac{1}{\lambda}(Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}^{\tau,\lambda\xi}}-Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}),\quad\text{strongly in $\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T])$}.

In particular,
\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}(s)
is the Gâteaux derivative of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}(s)
at
(\tau,t,\eta)
in the sense of (
28
).

To give an explicit representation of the vertical derivative
Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\cdot)
with respect to
\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}
in view of (
26
), we need to find out a measurable random field
U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\cdot):\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\mapsto\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T],\mathbb{R}^{d})
, such that for any
s\geq t
and
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{R}^{d}),

(229)

\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(s)=\bar{E}[U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\bar{\eta}_{t})(s)\bar{\xi}],

where
(\bar{\eta},\bar{\xi})
is an independent copy of
(\eta,\xi).

If (
229
) holds and moreover we show that
Y^{\gamma_{t},{\eta_{t}}}
is Fréchet differentiable with respect to
\eta_{t}
in the sense of (
24
) and Remark
2.9
, we have that

(230)

\partial_{\mu_{t}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(x_{t}):=U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(x_{t}),\ \ \forall\ x\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d},

is the vertical derivative of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}
at
\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}
. Here and in the following,
we write
\partial_{\mu}
instead of
\partial_{\mathcal{L}_{\eta}}
.
In view of (
200
) and (
229
), we formally deduce that
(U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(x_{t}),V^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(x_{t}))
solves the following BSDE: for any
s\in[t,T],

(231)

\begin{split}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(s)&=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dB(r),\end{split}

where
{U}^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}
solves the associated mean-field BSDE:

(232)

\begin{split}U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(s)&=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}})(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dB(r).\end{split}

According to Lemma
4.1
, we see that mean-field BSDE (
232
) is well posed with
(U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}},V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T],\mathbb{R}^{d})\times\mathbb{H}^{2}([t,T],\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}).
Then BSDE (
231
) also has a unique solution
(U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T],\mathbb{R}^{d})\times\mathbb{H}^{2}([t,T],\mathbb{R}^{d\times d})
. Moreover, according to the uniqueness of solutions for BSDEs (
232
), we see

U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}=U^{\gamma_{t},{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}|_{\gamma=\eta}.
Concerning the regularity of
U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
and
U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}
with respect to
(\gamma,\eta,x)
, we have

Lemma 4.11
.

For any
x,x^{\prime},\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d},
and
\eta,\eta^{\prime}\in\mathbb{M}_{2}^{D},
we have

(233)

\displaystyle\|U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}-U^{\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\leq C(\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|),

(234)

\displaystyle\|U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}-U^{\gamma^{\prime}_{t},\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\leq C(\|\gamma_{t}-\gamma^{\prime}_{t}\|+W_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}})+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|),

with
C
only depending on
\|\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}
.

Proof.

In the following we omit the subscript
t
and write
(U,V,Y,Z):=(U(r),V(r),Y(r),Z(r))
. Moreover, we only show the proof for (
233
) since (
234
) follows from (
233
) in a similar way. Set

\displaystyle(\Delta U,\Delta V):=(U^{\eta,x}-U^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}},V^{\eta,x}-V^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}}),

\displaystyle\Delta\partial_{\mu}\Phi:=\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta}},B^{x})-\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta^{\prime}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta^{\prime}}},B^{x^{\prime}}),

\displaystyle\Delta\partial_{\mu}f:=\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})-\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta^{\prime}}},\tilde{B}^{x^{\prime}_{t}}),

\displaystyle\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(1)}:=\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})-\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta^{\prime}}},\tilde{Y}^{x^{\prime}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}}),

\displaystyle\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(2)}:=\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})-\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta^{\prime}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\prime}_{t}}),

\displaystyle\Delta\partial_{(y,z)}f:=\partial_{(y,z)}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}})-\partial_{(y,z)}f(\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta^{\prime}}}),

\displaystyle\Delta\partial_{\omega}\tilde{Y}:=\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}-\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x^{\prime}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}}.

Then,
(\Delta U,\Delta V)
is the unique solution of BSDE

(235)

\begin{split}\Delta U(s)&=\Delta\partial_{\mu}\Phi+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta\partial_{\mu}f]dr+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}})\Delta\tilde{U}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}(\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}})\Delta U+\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}})\Delta V)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\Delta VdB(r)\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[(\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(1)})\partial_{\omega}\tilde{Y}^{x^{\prime},\tilde{\eta}^{\prime}}+(\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(2)})\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\prime},x^{\prime}}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}},\tilde{Y}^{x,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}})\Delta\partial_{\omega}\tilde{Y}^{x,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\left((\Delta\partial_{y}f)U^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}}+(\Delta\partial_{z}f)V^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}}\right)dr.\end{split}

By Lipschitz continuity of
(\partial_{(\mu,\nu,y,z)}f,\partial_{\mu}\Phi),
and boudnedness of
\partial_{(y,z)}f,
we see that

\displaystyle\|\Delta\partial_{\mu}\Phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta\partial_{\mu}f]dr\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\mathbb{E}[\int_{t}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}(|\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(1)}|^{2}+|\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(2)}|^{2})dr]

(236)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ +\mathbb{E}[\int_{t}^{T}|\Delta\partial_{y}f|^{2}dr]+\mathbb{E}[\int_{t}^{T}|\Delta\partial_{z}f|^{2}dr]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \leq C(\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|^{2}).

Moreover, since
\partial_{\omega}\tilde{Y}^{x^{\prime},\tilde{\eta}^{\prime}},\tilde{U}^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}},\tilde{Y}^{x,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
, and
V^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{H}^{2},
from the above estimate and the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we obtain

(237)

\begin{split}&\|\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(1)})\partial_{\omega}\tilde{Y}^{x^{\prime},\tilde{\eta}^{\prime}}+(\Delta\partial_{\nu}f^{(2)})\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\prime},x^{\prime}}\right]dr\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ +\|\int_{s}^{T}\left((\Delta\partial_{y}f)U^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}}+(\Delta\partial_{z}f)V^{\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime}}\right)dr\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \leq C(\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|).\end{split}

According to estimates given in Lemma
4.6
and boundedness of
\partial_{\nu}f,
we check that

(238)

\|\int_{s}^{T}|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta}},\tilde{Y}^{x,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}})\Delta\partial_{\omega}\tilde{Y}^{x,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}}]|dr\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|).

Then in view of Lemma
4.1
, inequalities (
236
), (
237
) and (
238
), we obtain the desired inequality (
233
).

∎

Remark 4.12
.

Similar to Lemma
4.3
, according to estimate (
234
),

U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}:=U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}

is well-defined.

Concerning the SVD
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}
of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}
at
(\tau,t,\mathcal{L}_{\eta})
,
\tau\leq t,
in view of Definition
2.10
and BSDE (
226
), we deduce that it is the unique solution of the following BSDE: for any
x\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d},

s\in[t,T],

(239)

\begin{split}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(s)&=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\Phi(B^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ -\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dB(r),\end{split}

where
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}
sloves the mean-field BSDE below

(240)

\begin{split}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(s)&=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}})(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dB(r).\end{split}

Thanks to Lemma
4.1
again, mean-field BSDE (
240
) has a unique solution
(\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}},\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{\eta_{t},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{H}^{2}.
Then the well-posedness of equation (
239
) follows similarly. Moreover we have that if
\tau=t,

(241)

\partial_{\mu_{t}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}=U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},\ \ \ \partial_{\mu_{t}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}=U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}},

and
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}=\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}|_{\gamma=\eta}.
Thus the following lemma follows similarly as Lemma
4.11
.

Lemma 4.13
.

For any
x,x^{\prime},\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d},
and
\eta,\eta^{\prime}\in\mathbb{M}_{2}^{D},
we have

(242)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}-\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\leq C(\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|),

(243)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}-\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma^{\prime}_{t},\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\leq C(\|\gamma_{t}-\gamma^{\prime}_{t}\|+W_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}})+\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|),

with
C
only depending on
\|\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}.

Recall that
\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}
is the solution of BSDE (
200
) and
U^{\gamma_{t},{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
solves equation (
231
).
The following lemma implies that
U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\cdot}:=U^{\gamma_{t},{\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is the derivative of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}
with respect to
\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}.

Lemma 4.14
.

For any
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{R}^{d}),
we have

(244)

\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(s)=\bar{E}[U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}],

where
(\bar{\eta},\bar{\xi})
is an independent copy of
(\eta,\xi)
.

Proof.

Substitute
\bar{\eta}_{t}
for
x_{t}
in equation (
232
) and multiply the equation by
\bar{\xi}
. Then we take the expectation
\bar{E}
on both sides of the relation, and obtain

(245)

\begin{split}\bar{E}[U^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}]&=\bar{E}\left[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\bar{\eta}_{t}})]\bar{\xi}\right]+\int_{s}^{T}\bar{E}\left[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\bar{\eta}_{t}})]\bar{\xi}\right]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\bar{E}[U^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\bar{E}[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\bar{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\bar{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]\bar{\xi}]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\bar{E}[\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]]dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\bar{E}[V^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]dr-\int_{s}^{T}\bar{E}[V^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]dB(r).\end{split}

Since random vectors
(\bar{\eta},\bar{\xi})
,
(\tilde{B},\tilde{Y},\tilde{U})
,
(B,Y,Z,\eta)
are mutually independent, and
(B^{\gamma_{t}},

Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}},

\partial_{x_{t}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}^{\eta_{t}}})
is independent of
\mathcal{F}_{t}
, we have

\begin{split}&\bar{E}\left[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\bar{\eta}_{t}})]\bar{\xi}\right]=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}],\\
&\bar{E}\left[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\bar{\eta}_{t}})]\bar{\xi}\right]=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}],\quad\text{and}\\
&\bar{E}\left[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\bar{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{x_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\bar{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]\bar{\xi}\right]=\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{x_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)\tilde{\xi}].\end{split}

Then identity (
245
) is equivalent to

\begin{split}\bar{E}[U^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}]=&\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\tilde{\xi}]dr\\
&\ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\bar{E}[U^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]dr-\int_{s}^{T}\bar{E}[V^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]dB(r)\\
&\ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)\tilde{\xi}]dr\\
&\ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\bar{E}[\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]]dr\\
&\ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\bar{E}[V^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(r)\bar{\xi}]dr,\end{split}

and therefore
(\bar{E}[U^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}],\bar{E}[V^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}])
satisfies BSDE (
201
). In view of uniqueness of solutions of BSDE (
201
), we see

\mathcal{Y}^{\eta_{t},\xi}=\bar{E}[U^{\eta_{t},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}].

Then identity (
244
) follows in a similar way.

∎

Theorem 4.15
.

Suppose that
(\Phi,f)
satisfies Assumption
(H1)
. For any
(t,\gamma,\eta)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\times\mathbb{M}^{D}_{2}
,
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}

is Fréchet differentiable with respect to
\eta_{t}
in the sense of (
24
) and Remark
2.9
. Moreover, the Fréchet derivative
D_{\eta_{t}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
has the following representation: for any
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{R}^{d}),

(246)

D_{\eta_{t}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}(s)(\xi)=\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(s)=\bar{E}[U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}],

where
\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}
is the solution of BSDE (
200
) and
U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},x_{t}},

x\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d},
is the solution of BSDE (
231
). In particular,
U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is the vertical derivative of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}
at
\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}
in the sense of (
26
) and Remark
2.9
.

Proof.

According to inequality (
206
) and argument therein, we see that
\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is a bounded linear operator from
L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{R}^{d})
to
\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T]).

Moreover, in view of Lemma
4.9
, for any
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{R}^{d}),

\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}
is the Gâteaux derivative of
Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
with respect to
\eta_{t}.
To show
\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is the Fréchet derivative of
Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
, it suffices to prove that
\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is continuous in
\eta_{t}\in\mathbb{S}^{2}([0,t])
as a linear bounded operator from
L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t})
to
\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T])
. Indeed, due to the representation (
244
) and estimate (
234
), we have that for any
\eta,\eta^{\prime}\in\mathbb{M}_{2}^{D},

\|\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}-\mathcal{Y}^{\gamma_{t},\eta^{\prime}_{t},\xi}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{2}=\mathbb{E}\|\bar{E}[U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}]-\bar{E}[U^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}},\bar{\eta}^{\prime}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}]\|^{2}\leq C\|\bar{\xi}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\bar{\eta}_{t}-\bar{\eta}^{\prime}_{t}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}.

Thus we have the following estimate and complete our proof

\|\mathcal{Y}^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\cdot}-\mathcal{Y}^{\gamma_{t},\eta^{\prime}_{t},\cdot}\|_{L(L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{t}),\mathbb{S}^{2})}\leq C\|\bar{\eta}_{t}-\bar{\eta}^{\prime}_{t}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}.

∎

For the strong vertical differentiability of
Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
at
(\tau,t,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}),
similar as the proof of Lemma
4.14
, we have that for any
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{\tau},\mathbb{R}^{d}),

(247)

\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(s)=\bar{E}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}].

Moreover, the following proposition implies that
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\cdot}
is the SVD of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}
at
(\tau,t,\mathcal{L}_{\eta}),
the proof of which follows from Lemma
4.10
, Lemma
4.13
and identity (
247
).

Proposition 4.16
.

For any
(t,\gamma,\eta)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\times\mathbb{M}^{D}_{2}
and
\tau\in[0,t]
,
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}

is Fréchet differentiable with respect to
\eta_{\tau}
in the sense of (
27
) and Remark
2.11
. Moreover, the Fréchet derivative
D_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
has the representation: for any
\xi\in L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{\tau},\mathbb{R}^{d})

(248)

D_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}(s)(\xi)=\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}(s)=\bar{E}[\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\bar{\eta}_{t}}(s)\bar{\xi}],

where
\partial_{\eta_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},\xi}
is the solution of BSDE (
226
) and
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},x_{t}},

x\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d},
is the solution of BSDE (
239
).

4.2.
Second-order differentiability

In this section, results are written in
d=1
case for simplicity of notations.
For the second order differentiability of
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}
, we assume that
(\Phi,f)
satisfies assumption
(H2)
.

According to Proposition
4.6
,
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}
is strongly vertically differentiable at
(t,\gamma),
and the derivative
\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
at
(\tau,t,\gamma)
solves the linear BSDE (
196
). Similarly, in view of
(H2)
, we see that
\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
is strongly vertically differentiable at
(\tau,t,\gamma),
and moreover, the derivative
\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}^{2}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}
is the unique solution of BSDE in the form of (
173
).
To apply Theorem
2.16
on
Y^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}
, it remains to study
the differentiability of
\partial_{\mu_{t}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}}(x_{t})=U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
with respect to
x_{t}\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d}
. Since
U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
is the unique solution of BSDE (
231
), by formally taking vertical derivative at
(t,x),
we obtain the following linear BSDE: for
s\in[t,T],

(249)

\begin{split}&\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(s)\\
&\ \ =\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})^{T}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial^{2}_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)\ dB(r),\end{split}

where
\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}{U}^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}
solves a mean-field linear BSDE

(250)

\begin{split}&\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(s)\\
&\ \ =\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})^{T}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial^{2}_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)\ dB(r).\end{split}

Lemma 4.17
.

There exist unique solutions
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}([t,T])\times\mathbb{H}^{2}([t,T])
and
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{p}([t,T])\times\mathbb{H}^{p}([t,T])
of equations (
250
) and (
249
), respectively. Moreover,
\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
is the vertical derivative of
U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
at
(t,x)
, and for any
K>0
and
(\gamma,\eta,x),\ (\gamma^{\prime},\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime})\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\times\mathbb{M}_{2}^{D}\times\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\text{ such that }|||\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}|||,|||\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}|||\leq K,

(251)

\begin{split}&\|(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}})\|_{\mathbb{S}^{p}\times\mathbb{H}^{p}}\leq C_{p},\\
&\|(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}-\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\gamma^{\prime}_{t},\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}-\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{\gamma^{\prime}_{t},\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}})\|_{\mathbb{S}^{p}\times\mathbb{H}^{p}}\\
&\ \ \ \leq C_{K,p}(\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|+W_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}})+\|\gamma_{t}-\gamma^{\prime}_{t}\|),\end{split}

with some constants
C_{p}
and
C_{K,p}.

Proof.

To show
the well-posedness of (
250
), according to Lemma
4.1
, it remains to check the following terms belong to
L^{2}(\mathcal{F}_{T})
,

\displaystyle\Big|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]\Big|,\ \int_{t}^{T}\Big|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]\Big|dr,

\displaystyle\int_{t}^{T}\Big|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial^{2}_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]\Big|dr,\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle\int_{t}^{T}\Big|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\partial_{\omega_{t}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r))^{T}]\Big|,

which follows easily by the boundedness of
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi,\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}f,\partial_{\nu}f,\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f)
and Proposition
4.6
.
Moreover, we have

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}\leq C_{p},\\
&\|\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}-\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}-\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}\leq C_{K,p}(\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|+\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}).\end{split}

Concerning the well-posedness of (
249
), since
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{\eta_{t},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{\eta_{t},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{H}^{2}
, we have

\int_{t}^{T}\Big|\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\tilde{U}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]\Big|dr\in L^{p}(\mathcal{F}_{T}),

and therefore, there exists a unique solution
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}U^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}V^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}})\in\mathbb{S}^{p}\times\mathbb{H}^{p}.
In view of the boundedness of
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}\Phi,\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{t}}\partial_{\mu_{t}}f,\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f,\partial_{y}f,\partial_{z}f)
and standard estimate for BSDEs, we have our desired estimates.
∎

Recall that
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
is the solution of BSDE (
239
).
To prove the strong vertical differentiability of
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
at
(\tau,t,x)
, we consider BSDE

(252)

\begin{split}&\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(s)\\
&\ \ =\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\Phi(B^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r))^{T}]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial^{2}_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ -\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}(r)\ dB(r),\ s\in[t,T],\end{split}

where
\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}
solves the following mean-field linear BSDE

(253)

\begin{split}&\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(s)\\
&\ \ =\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\Phi(B^{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]+\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{B}^{x_{t}})]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{y}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\tilde{y}}\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r))^{T}]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}})\partial^{2}_{\omega_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{x_{t},\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\partial_{\nu}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t},x_{t}}(r)]dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{z}f(\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}^{\eta_{t}}})\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{\eta_{t},x_{t}}(r)\ dB(r).\end{split}

Then we have the following lemma via a similar proof of Lemma
4.17
.

Lemma 4.18
.

There exists a unique solution
(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}})
of BSDE (
252
). Moreover,
\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
is the SVD of
\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}
at
(\tau,t,x)
, and
for any
K>0
,

(254)

\begin{split}&\|(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}})\|_{\mathbb{S}^{p}\times\mathbb{H}^{p}}\leq C_{p},\ \ \ \ \ \\
&\|(\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}-\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Y^{\gamma_{t}^{\prime},\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}},\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}},x_{t}}-\partial_{\tilde{\omega}_{\tau}}\partial_{\mu_{\tau}}Z^{\gamma_{t}^{\prime},\eta^{\prime}_{t},x^{\prime}_{t}})\|_{\mathbb{S}^{p}\times\mathbb{H}^{p}}\\
&\ \ \ \leq C_{K,p}(\|x_{t}-x^{\prime}_{t}\|+W_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}})+\|\gamma_{t}-\gamma^{\prime}_{t}\|),\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \forall\ (\gamma,\eta,x),\ (\gamma^{\prime},\eta^{\prime},x^{\prime})\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\times\mathbb{M}_{2}^{D}\times\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\text{ such that }|||\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}}|||,|||\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}|||\leq K,\end{split}

with some constants
C_{p}
and
C_{K,p}.

5.
Appendix

5.1.
Proof of Lemma
4.3

We omit the proof of inequality (
186
) since it is similar to that of (
187
) for
p=2.

Now suppose that (
186
) is true, and we show inquality (
188
) first. In what follows, we write
(\mathcal{Y},\mathcal{Z}):=(Y^{\eta_{t}},Z^{\eta_{t}})
and notations such as
(\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime})
and
(\Phi,\Phi^{\prime})
are defined in a similar way. Set
(\delta\mathcal{Y},\delta\mathcal{Z}):=(\mathcal{Y}-\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}-\mathcal{Z}^{\prime})
. We see that
(\delta\mathcal{Y},\delta\mathcal{Z})
solves the following linearized BSDE

(255)

\begin{split}\delta\mathcal{Y}(s)&=\Phi-\Phi^{\prime}+\int_{s}^{T}[f(\Theta^{\eta}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta}_{r}})-f(\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}}_{r}})]dr-\int_{s}^{T}\delta\mathcal{Z}(r)dB(r)\\
&=:\delta\Phi+\int_{s}^{T}\Big(a_{r}\delta\mathcal{Y}(r)+b_{r}\delta\mathcal{Z}(r)+\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{c}_{r}\delta\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}(r)]+\delta h_{r}\Big)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\delta\mathcal{Z}(r)dB(r),\end{split}

where

\displaystyle a_{r}:=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{y}{f}(B^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime}+\theta(\mathcal{Y}-\mathcal{Y}^{\prime}),\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta}_{r}})d\theta,

\displaystyle b_{r}:=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{z}{f}(B^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}+\theta(\mathcal{Z}-\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r}})d\theta,

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{r}:=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\nu}f(B^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{Y}^{\prime}+\theta(\mathcal{Y}-\mathcal{Y}^{\prime})},\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{\prime}+\theta(\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}-\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{\prime}))d\theta,\quad\text{and}\quad

\displaystyle\delta h_{r}:=f(B^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}_{r}},\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{\prime}})-f(B^{{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}}_{r},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}_{r}},\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{\prime}}).

Let

F(\Theta^{\eta}_{t},y_{1},y_{2}):=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\nu}f(B^{\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{Y}^{\prime}+\theta(\mathcal{Y}-\mathcal{Y}^{\prime})},y_{1}+\theta y_{2})d\theta.

Then,
F
is uniformly Lipschitz continuous in
(y_{1},y_{2})
in view of Assumption
(H0)
. On the other hand, since
\mathcal{Y}^{\prime},\mathcal{Z}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{H}^{2},
we deduce that
F(\Theta^{\eta}_{t},0,0)\in\mathbb{H}^{2}
, and moreover, we have

(256)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Y}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}+\|\mathcal{Y}^{\prime}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}+\|F(\Theta^{\eta}_{t},0,0)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}\leq C(1+\|\eta_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}).

Then applying estimates of Lemma
4.1
to BSDE (
255
), we have

(257)

\|\delta\mathcal{Y}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+\|\delta\mathcal{Z}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}\leq C(\|\Phi-\Phi^{\prime}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\delta h\|^{2}_{\mathbb{H}^{2}})e^{C(\|F(\Theta^{\eta}_{t},0,0)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}+\|(\mathcal{Y},\mathcal{Y}^{\prime})\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}})}.

Furthermore, using the Lipschitz continuity of
\Phi
and
f,
we have

(258)

\|\Phi-\Phi^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\delta h\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}\leq C\|\eta_{t}-\eta^{\prime}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{2}},

and thus the desired estimate (
188
) in view of inequality (
257
).

Now we show inequalities (
187
). In what follows, we omit the superscript
(\gamma_{t},\eta_{t})
for simplicity.
Without loss of generality, we assume
p=2q,\ q\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}.
Otherwise, we replace
|Y|
with
(|Y|^{2}+\varepsilon)^{\frac{1}{2}}
in the following argument and then take the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
. Applying Itô’s formula to
|Y|^{p}
on
[s,T],
we have

(259)

\begin{split}&|Y(s)|^{p}+\frac{1}{2}p(p-1)\int_{s}^{T}|Y|^{p-2}|Z|^{2}dr\\
&\quad=\ |\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}+p\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p-1}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r}})dr-p\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p-1}Z(r)dB(r).\end{split}

Since
\Phi
and
f
are Lipschitz continuous, we have

\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}}),\int_{t}^{T}f(B_{r}^{\gamma_{t}},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta_{t}}(r)})dr\in L^{q}(\mathcal{F}_{T}),\quad\forall q\geq 1.

Then, using standard estimates of BSDEs, we obtain

\|Y\|_{\mathbb{S}^{q}}+\|Z\|_{\mathbb{H}^{q}}<\infty.

Taking the expectation on both sides of identity (
259
), we have

(260)

\mathbb{E}[|Y(s)|^{p}+\frac{1}{2}p(p-1)\int_{s}^{T}|Y|^{p-2}|Z|^{2}dr]\leq\mathbb{E}[|\Phi|^{p}]+p\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p-1}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r}})dr.

Applying Young’s inequality to the last integral, we have

(261)

\begin{split}&\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p-1}f(\Theta^{\gamma_{t},\eta_{t}}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r}})dr\\
&\ \ \leq\int_{s}^{T}\Big[|Y|^{p-1}f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})dr+C|Y|^{p}+C|Y|^{p-1}[\tilde{\mathbb{E}}[|\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}(r)|^{2}]]^{\frac{1}{2}}+C|Y|^{p-1}|Z|\Big]dr\\
&\ \ \leq(C_{p}+\frac{C}{\varepsilon})\int_{s}^{T}|Y|^{p}dr+\frac{1}{p}\int_{s}^{T}|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr\\
&\ \ \ \ \ \ +\varepsilon C\int_{s}^{T}|Y|^{p-2}|Z|^{2}dr+\frac{C}{2p}\int_{s}^{T}\|\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}(r)\|_{L^{2}}^{p}dr,\quad\forall\varepsilon>0.\end{split}

Then by choosing a small enough
\varepsilon
such that
\frac{1}{2}p(p-1)-\varepsilon C>0
, we obtain

(262)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}[|Y(s)|^{p}+C_{p}\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p-2}|Z(r)|^{2}dr]\\
&\quad\leq\mathbb{E}[|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}]+C_{p}\int_{s}^{T}[|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr+\int_{s}^{T}|Y|^{p}dr]\\
&\quad\quad+\int_{s}^{T}\|\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}(r)\|_{L^{2}}^{p}dr.\end{split}

Apply Gronwall’s inequality to (
262
), and we obtain

(263)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}[|Y(s)|^{p}+C_{p}\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p-2}|Z(r)|^{2}dr]\\
&\ \ \ \leq C_{p}\mathbb{E}[|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}+\int_{s}^{T}|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr]+\int_{s}^{T}\|\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}_{t}}(r)\|_{L^{2}}^{p}dr.\end{split}

Then in view of inequalities (
261
) and (
259
), choosing
\varepsilon
sufficiently small, we have

(264)

\begin{split}|Y(s)|^{p}&\leq|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}+C_{p}\Big[\int_{s}^{T}|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr\\
&\ \ \ +\int_{s}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p}dr+\int_{s}^{T}\|Y^{\eta_{t}}(r)\|^{p}_{L_{2}}dr\Big]-p\int_{s}^{T}|Y|^{p-1}Z(r)dB(r).\end{split}

Applying Burkholder-Davis-Gundy inequality to the right hand side of inequality (
264
), we obtain

(265)

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\sup_{s\in[t,T]}|Y(s)|^{p}]\leq&\ \mathbb{E}\left[|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}\right]+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{t}^{T}|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr\\
&+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{s}^{T}\|Y^{\eta_{t}}(r)\|^{p}_{L_{2}}dr+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{t}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p}dr\\
&+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\Big(\int_{t}^{T}|Y|^{2p-2}|Z|^{2}dr\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\\
\leq&\ \mathbb{E}\left[|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}\right]+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{t}^{T}|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr\\
&+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{s}^{T}\|Y^{\eta_{t}}(r)\|^{p}_{L_{2}}dr+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{t}^{T}|Y(r)|^{p}dr\\
&+\varepsilon\,\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\in[t,T]}|Y(s)|^{p}\right]+\frac{1}{4\varepsilon}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}|Y|^{p-2}|Z|^{2}dr\right].\end{split}

Then in view of (
263
), (
186
) and (
265
), for sufficiently small
\varepsilon
, we have

(266)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\in[t,T]}|Y(s)|^{p}\right]\\
\leq&\ \ C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\left[|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}\right]+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\int_{t}^{T}|f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},0,0,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\delta_{0})|^{p}dr\\
&\ \ +C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{s}^{T}\|Y^{\eta_{t}}(r)\|^{p}_{L_{2}}dr\right]\\
\leq&\ \ C_{p}\left(1+\mathbb{E}[\|B^{\gamma_{t}}\|^{p}]+\|\|B^{\eta_{t}}\|\|_{L_{2}}^{p}+\|\|\eta_{t}\|\|^{p}_{L_{2}}\right)\\
\leq&\ \ C_{p}\left(1+\mathbb{E}\left[(\|B\|+\|\gamma_{t}\|)^{p}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[(\|B\|+\|\eta_{t}\|)^{2}\right]^{\frac{p}{2}}+\|\|\eta_{t}\|\|^{p}_{L_{2}}\right)\\
\leq&\ \ C_{p}\left(1+\|\gamma_{t}\|^{p}+\|\|\eta_{t}\|\|^{p}_{L_{2}}\right).\end{split}

Let
\tilde{f}(Y,Z):=f(B_{r}^{\gamma_{t}},Y,Z,\mathcal{L}_{B_{r}^{\eta_{t}}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta_{t}}(r)})
. Using a standard argument of BSDEs, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[|\int_{t}^{T}|Z|^{2}dr|^{\frac{p}{2}}]\leq&\,C_{p}\mathbb{E}\left[|\Phi(B_{T}^{\gamma_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{B_{T}^{\eta_{t}}})|^{p}+\int_{t}^{T}|\tilde{f}(0,0)|^{p}dr+C_{p}\int_{s}^{T}\|Y(r)\|^{p}_{L_{2}}dr\right]\\
&\,+C_{p}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\in[t,T]}|Y(s)|^{p}\right]\leq\,C_{p}(1+\|\gamma_{t}\|^{p}+\|\|\eta_{t}\|\|^{p}_{L_{2}}),\end{split}

and thus (
187
).

It remains to prove (
189
). Note that
(\delta Y,\delta Z):=(Y-Y^{\prime},Z-Z^{\prime})
solves the following linearized BSDE

(267)

\begin{split}\delta Y(s)&=\Phi-\Phi^{\prime}+\int_{s}^{T}[f(\Theta_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta_{t}}_{r}})-f(\Theta^{\prime}_{r},\mathcal{L}_{\Theta^{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}_{r}})]dr-\int_{s}^{T}\delta Z(r)dB(r)\\
&=:\delta\Phi+\int_{s}^{T}\Big(\alpha_{r}\delta Y(r)+\beta_{r}\delta Z(r)+\delta h_{r}+\delta f_{r}\Big)dr-\int_{s}^{T}\delta Z(r)dB(r),\end{split}

where

\displaystyle\alpha_{r}:=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{y}{f}(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},Y^{\prime}+\theta(Y-Y^{\prime}),Z,\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}})d\theta,

\displaystyle\beta_{r}:=\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{z}{f}(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},Y^{\prime},Z+\theta(Z-Z^{\prime}),\mathcal{L}_{\Theta_{r}})d\theta,\ \ \delta\tilde{Y}^{\eta}:=\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}}-\tilde{Y}^{\tilde{\eta}^{\prime}},

\displaystyle\delta{h}_{r}:=f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},Y^{\prime},Z^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}_{r}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta_{t}}})-f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},Y^{\prime},Z^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}_{r}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}}),\quad\text{and}\quad

\displaystyle\delta f_{r}:=f(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r},Y^{\prime},Z^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta_{t}}_{r}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}})-f(B^{\gamma^{\prime}_{t}}_{r},Y^{\prime},Z^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{B^{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}_{r}},\mathcal{L}_{Y^{\eta^{\prime}_{t}}}).

Using the Lipschitz continuity of
f
in
(y,z)
and standard estimate for linear BSDEs (see e.g.
[
4
]
), we have

(268)

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sup_{s\in[t,T]}|\delta Y(s)|^{p}+(\int_{t}^{T}|\delta Z(s)|^{2}ds)^{\frac{p}{2}}\Big]\leq C_{p}\Big(\mathbb{E}|\delta\Phi|^{p}+\mathbb{E}\Big|\int_{t}^{T}(\delta h_{r}+\delta f_{r})dr\Big|^{p}\Big).

Then, in view of estimate (
188
), we have

(269)

\mathbb{E}|\delta\Phi|^{p}+\mathbb{E}|\int_{t}^{T}\delta h_{r}dr|^{p}+\mathbb{E}|\int_{t}^{T}\delta f_{r}dr|^{p}\leq C_{K}(\|\gamma_{t}-\gamma^{\prime}_{t}\|^{p}+W_{2}(\mathcal{L}_{\eta_{t}},\mathcal{L}_{\eta^{\prime}_{t}})^{p}),

and thus the desired estimate (
189
).

5.2.
An extension of
[
46
, Theorem 4.5]
without assumption of local Lipschitz continuity in time

Lemma 5.1
.

Let non-anticipative functional
f:[0,T]\times\mathbb{D}_{T,d}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
lie in
\mathscr{C}^{0,2,2,2}_{s}.
Assume that for any
t\in[0,T]
and
\tau\leq t,

f
and all its derivatives are locally Lipschitz continuous on
\mathbb{D}_{T,d}
: for
\phi(t,\cdot)=(I,\partial_{\omega_{\tau}},\partial_{\omega_{\tau}}^{2})f(t,\cdot,0,0),

(270)

|\phi(t,\omega)-\phi(t,\omega^{\prime})|\leq C(1+\|\omega_{t}\|^{k}+\|\omega^{\prime}_{t}\|^{k})(\|\omega_{t}-\omega^{\prime}_{t}\|),\quad\forall\ (\omega,\omega^{\prime})\in\mathbb{D}_{T,d}^{2},

for some constant
C
and integer
k.
Moreover, suppose that the first-order derivatives in
(y,z)
, as well as their first-order derivatives w.r.t.
(\omega_{\tau},y,z)
are uniformly bounded. If
\Phi
satisfies (
195
), there is a unique classical solution of the following PPDE

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{t}u(t,\gamma)+\frac{1}{2}\text{Tr}\ [\partial_{\omega}^{2}u(t,\gamma)]+f(t,\gamma,u(t,\gamma),\partial_{\omega}u(t,\gamma))=0,\\
\\
u(T,\gamma)=\Phi(\gamma_{T}),\ \ \ \ (t,\gamma)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{C}_{T,d}.\end{array}\right.

Proof.

The uniqueness is a consequence of that of the following non-Markovian BSDE

(275)

Y^{\gamma_{t}}(s)=\Phi(B^{\gamma_{t}}_{T})+\int_{s}^{T}f(r,B^{\gamma_{t}},Y^{\gamma_{t}}(r),Z^{\gamma_{t}}(r))dr-\int_{s}^{T}Z^{\gamma_{t}}(r)dB(r).

We now sketch the proof of the existence. Set

(276)

u(t,\gamma):=Y^{\gamma_{t}}(t).

Similar to that of
[
46
, Theorems 3.9 and 3.10]
, we have
u\in\mathscr{C}^{0,2}_{s,p}
and moreover,

(277)

u(s,B^{\gamma_{t}})=Y^{\gamma_{t}}(s),\quad\partial_{\omega_{t}}u(s,B^{\gamma_{t}})=Z^{\gamma_{t}}(s),\quad s\geq t.

Then, applying the partial Itô formula (
82
), we have that for any
\delta>0,

\displaystyle u(t+\delta,\gamma_{t})-u(t,\gamma_{t})

\displaystyle\quad=u(t+\delta,\gamma_{t})-\mathbb{E}[u(t+\delta,B^{\gamma_{t}})]+\mathbb{E}[u(t+\delta,B^{\gamma_{t}})]-u(t,\gamma_{t})

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[-\int_{t}^{t+\delta}\partial_{\omega_{r}}u(t+\delta,B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r})dB(r)-\frac{1}{2}\int_{t}^{t+\delta}\text{Tr}[\partial_{\omega_{r}}^{2}u(t+\delta,B^{\gamma_{t}}_{r})]dr\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[-\int_{t}^{t+\delta}f(r,B^{\gamma_{t}},Y^{\gamma_{t}}(r),Z^{\gamma_{t}}(r))dr\right].

Dividing both sides of the above identity by
\delta
and taking
\delta\rightarrow 0,
we complete the proof.

∎
